独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37238852759 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

F2 · Registry 图片预览不报告解码失败，损坏图片只显示破图

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx 的 image 分支直接渲染 <img>，无 onError/失败回退；仅 OOXML 路径有 viewerError 与 DownloadFallback。应用副本新增 ImagePreview(onError) 后 B06 才有明确失败反馈。

实际影响
按基线安装预览组件时，MIME 为 image 但字节损坏的文件只会显示破图，与“损坏时要说明情况”的验收不一致；Agent 需改 Registry 源码或写应用扩展。
建议改法
在基线 image 分支加入 onError → DownloadFallback（与 OOXML 一致），或在 lib 层提供可覆盖的失败渲染。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
预览 MIME/扩展名为 image 但字节损坏的文件（B06 场景）
应有能力 / 行为
预览组件在图片解码失败时显示明确失败信息并可选下载
实际观察
registry 基线 image 分支无 onError，破损内容静默显示为破图
应用绕行与代价
应用在 client/extensions 副本中新增 ImagePreview(onError)+DownloadFallback
改进后的验收标准
用基线组件预览损坏 PNG 时显示失败提示并可选下载，可用截图或 DOM 判定。

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:71–79E19

resolveFilePreviewKind 的 image 分支直接返回裸 <img src alt className>，没有 onError 或失败回退；损坏图片只会显示破图。

  switch (kind) {
    case 'image':
      return (
        <img
          src={url}
          alt={file.filename}
          className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
        />
      );
app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:96–131E29

应用扩展副本新增 ImagePreview：useState(failed)+<img onError={()=>setFailed(true)}>，失败时渲染 DownloadFallback 并给出 previewFailed 文案，弥补注册表基线图片分支缺少解码失败反馈。

/**
 * An image preview that reports a decode failure instead of leaving a broken
 * image behind. A file can be stored with an image MIME type and valid PNG
 * header while its bytes are corrupt, so `onError` is the only signal that the
 * content cannot be shown; it turns that into the same explicit message and
 * download fallback the other unrenderable kinds already use.
 */
function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
  readonly onDownload?: () => void;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url, onDownload } = inputProps;
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  if (failed || !url) {
    return (
      <DownloadFallback
        file={file}
        message={t('previewFailed', {
          defaultValue: 'Unable to load the file preview.',
        })}
        onDownload={onDownload}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:248–253E24

SKILL 列出错误码恢复（BODY_TOO_LARGE/INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION/STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE 等），并声明没有内建路由认证、行级 ACL、Range/206、ETag、条件下载、断点续传、物理清理、内容嗅探或病毒扫描，需业务自行实现。

- BODY_TOO_LARGE (413): reduce request size or adjust route limits. INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILES (400): send native File values and a nonempty batch.
- INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION: first compare the migration name, route collection and Server repository argument; then await collections.get(logicalName) and check the active connection and generated metadata. SQLite physical text does not imply a broken datetime definition. Fix the specific lookup, metadata or schema problem and retry the same upload; do not bypass File Repository. Use a new migration for corrections to already-merged history.
- STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE: configure a capable disk or stream mode.
- FILE_COMMIT_UNCERTAIN / FILE_CLEANUP_FAILED: reconcile database records and stored objects before retrying; uploads have no idempotency key.

There is no built-in route authentication, row ACL, Range/206, ETag, conditional download, resumable upload, physical cleanup, content sniffing or malware scan. Metadata deletion retains objects; cancelled forms may leave unlinked files. Implement the policies required by the business, including referenced-file deletion and orphan cleanup.
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][能力缺口] Registry 图片预览不报告解码失败，损坏图片只显示破图

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-file`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.16）、`packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md`

registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx 的 image 分支直接渲染 <img>，无 onError/失败回退；仅 OOXML 路径有 viewerError 与 DownloadFallback。应用副本新增 ImagePreview(onError) 后 B06 才有明确失败反馈。

**影响**：按基线安装预览组件时，MIME 为 image 但字节损坏的文件只会显示破图，与“损坏时要说明情况”的验收不一致；Agent 需改 Registry 源码或写应用扩展。

**建议改动**：在基线 image 分支加入 onError → DownloadFallback（与 OOXML 一致），或在 lib 层提供可覆盖的失败渲染。

**验收标准**：用基线组件预览损坏 PNG 时显示失败提示并可选下载，可用截图或 DOM 判定。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:71–79` — resolveFilePreviewKind 的 image 分支直接返回裸 <img src alt className>，没有 onError 或失败回退；损坏图片只会显示破图。
- `app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:96–131` — 应用扩展副本新增 ImagePreview：useState(failed)+<img onError={()=>setFailed(true)}>，失败时渲染 DownloadFallback 并给出 previewFailed 文案，弥补注册表基线图片分支缺少解码失败反馈。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:248–253` — SKILL 列出错误码恢复（BODY_TOO_LARGE/INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION/STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE 等），并声明没有内建路由认证、行级 ACL、Range/206、ETag、条件下载、断点续传、物理清理、内容嗅探或病毒扫描，需业务自行实现。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37238852759 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

F1 · DatabaseManager 与 DatabaseConnection 的 repository 签名不一致，正常装配触发类型冲突

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/db · 原评审：未解决

⌄

@nocobase/db 的 DatabaseConnection.repository(collection) 与 DatabaseManager.repository(collection, connection?) 同名但参数不同；把 databaseManagerToken 解析出的 Manager 传给期望 Connection 的 helper 时，verify-2 日志第 271/275/292/419 行均报 TS2345（Manager 缺少 name/driver/dialect 等 8+ 属性）。

实际影响
按“数据库对象.repository()”直觉装配的 Agent 需回退到 connection.repository() 或自定义 Pick<DatabaseManager,"repository">，增加排查与改造成本，并阻塞 typecheck 门禁；运行时行为本身未被证明错误。
建议改法
为两者抽出共享 RepositoryAccessor 接口，或在 Manager 上提供显式 connectionRepository(name) 并从类型上区分；同时在 nocobase-db SKILL 说明 Manager 与 Connection 的 repository 差异。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
在 server/providers/materials.ts 把 DatabaseManager 直接传给期望 DatabaseConnection 的 repository 访问器
应有能力 / 行为
Manager 与 Connection 的 repository 访问在类型上可互换，或差异被显式标注并可被类型/报错定位
实际观察
verify-2.log 报 TS2345：DatabaseManager 不能赋给 DatabaseConnection（缺 name/driver/dialect/schemaManagement 等 8+ 属性）
应用绕行与代价
应用改用 Pick<DatabaseManager,"repository"> 并显式取 connection（retro 记录），未修改框架
改进后的验收标准
同一 helper 同时接受 Manager 与 Connection 并通过 tsc --noEmit，且 SKILL 有对应说明。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/connection.d.ts:11–49E13

DatabaseConnection 界面声明 repository<T>(collection: string)（单参）、withPolicies/transaction、builder/collections/query 等；repository 只按 collection 取。

export interface DatabaseConnection {
    name: string;
    driver: DatabaseDriver;
    /** Dialect identifier supplied by the registered driver package. */
    dialect: string;
    schemaManagement: SchemaManagementMode;
    capabilities: DatabaseCapabilities;
    /** Runtime strategies supplied by the registered dialect package. */
    runtime: DatabaseDriverRuntime;
    /** Collection schema and metadata builder. Uses Collection and Field logical names. */
    builder: CollectionBuilder;
    /** Resolved physical Schema plus supplemental Collection metadata. */
    collections: ConnectionCollections;
    /** Supplemental Collection metadata read and update service. */
    collectionMetadata: CollectionMetadataService;
    /** Database-layer query builder. Uses Connection naming but not Collection-level overrides. */
    query: QueryAdapter;
    repository<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord, TCreate extends object = Partial<TRecord>, TUpdate extends object = Partial<TRecord>>(collection: string): Repository<TRecord, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    /**
     * Bind a Policy per Collection for one principal.
     *
     * Binding lives here rather than on each Repository because a request
     * usually touches several Collections and takes fresh Repositories inside a
     * transaction; one place to bind is also one place to see what this request
     * was authorized to touch.
     */
    withPolicies<P>(policies: Readonly<Record<string, RepositoryPolicy | ((principal: P) => RepositoryPolicy)>>, principal: P): ScopedDatabaseConnection;
    schema: SchemaAdapter;
    /** Read-only physical database schema introspection. Uses physical names. */
    schemaInspector: SchemaInspector;
    /** Escape hatch for the underlying adapter client. Prefer builder/query for portable code. */
    client<T = unknown>(): Promise<T>;
    connect(): Promise<this>;
    disconnect(): Promise<void>;
    reconnect(): Promise<this>;
    /** Destructively clears the objects owned by this managed connection. */
    resetManagedSchema(): Promise<void>;
    transaction<T>(fn: (connection: DatabaseConnection) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/manager.d.ts:23–44E14

DatabaseManager 同时声明 repository<T>(collection: string, connection?: string)（双参，第二参为连接名）以及 connection(name)、builder(name)、collections(name) 等，与 DatabaseConnection 的同名 repository 签名不同。

export interface DatabaseManager {
    connection(name?: string): DatabaseConnection;
    /** Collection schema and metadata builder. Uses Collection and Field logical names. */
    builder(name?: string): CollectionBuilder;
    /** Database-layer query builder. Does not read Collection metadata or collection table prefixes. */
    query(name?: string): QueryAdapter;
    /**
     * Resolved Collections of one connection, by logical name. Same object as
     * `connection(name).collections`, so its cache is shared with every Builder,
     * Repository and Migration on that connection: `invalidate()` and
     * `refresh()` affect all of them.
     */
    collections(name?: string): ConnectionCollections;
    repository<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord, TCreate extends object = Partial<TRecord>, TUpdate extends object = Partial<TRecord>>(collection: string, connection?: string): Repository<TRecord, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    createMigrator(options: DatabaseMigratorOptions): Migrator;
    createSeeder(options: DatabaseSeederOptions): Seeder;
    connect(name?: string): Promise<DatabaseConnection>;
    transaction<T>(fn: (connection: DatabaseConnection) => Promise<T>, name?: string): Promise<T>;
    disconnect(name?: string): Promise<void>;
    reconnect(name?: string): Promise<DatabaseConnection>;
    destroy(): Promise<void>;
}

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][易用性改进] DatabaseManager 与 DatabaseConnection 的 repository 签名不一致，正常装配触发类型冲突

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.16）

@nocobase/db 的 DatabaseConnection.repository(collection) 与 DatabaseManager.repository(collection, connection?) 同名但参数不同；把 databaseManagerToken 解析出的 Manager 传给期望 Connection 的 helper 时，verify-2 日志第 271/275/292/419 行均报 TS2345（Manager 缺少 name/driver/dialect 等 8+ 属性）。

**影响**：按“数据库对象.repository()”直觉装配的 Agent 需回退到 connection.repository() 或自定义 Pick<DatabaseManager,"repository">，增加排查与改造成本，并阻塞 typecheck 门禁；运行时行为本身未被证明错误。

**建议改动**：为两者抽出共享 RepositoryAccessor 接口，或在 Manager 上提供显式 connectionRepository(name) 并从类型上区分；同时在 nocobase-db SKILL 说明 Manager 与 Connection 的 repository 差异。

**验收标准**：同一 helper 同时接受 Manager 与 Connection 并通过 tsc --noEmit，且 SKILL 有对应说明。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/connection.d.ts:11–49` — DatabaseConnection 界面声明 repository<T>(collection: string)（单参）、withPolicies/transaction、builder/collections/query 等；repository 只按 collection 取。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/manager.d.ts:23–44` — DatabaseManager 同时声明 repository<T>(collection: string, connection?: string)（双参，第二参为连接名）以及 connection(name)、builder(name)、collections(name) 等，与 DatabaseConnection 的同名 repository 签名不同。
- `artifacts/verify-2.log:12–19` — 工厂 typecheck 日志：server/providers/materials.ts(271,39)/(275,33)/(292,38)/(419,20) 均报 TS2345，DatabaseManager 不能赋给 DatabaseConnection（缺 name/driver/dialect/schemaManagement 等 8+ 属性），最终 exit 2。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37238852759 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
业务实现与执行环境观察 · 不计入框架得分（1）
轻微指引错误
F3 · verify-3 在 repair-3 之前完成，最终 QA 实际反映修复前快照

工厂流程 · 原评审：未解决

⌄

QA 观察（/api/project_materials 404、无 materials 页面）与 repair-3 新增文件之间存在明确时间先后，最终验收轮缺失。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

repair-summary.json 记录 verificationAttempts=3、repairAttempts=3、finalVerificationAttempt=3，配合 verify-3 报告 B01–B06 全 failed，容易被读作对最终代码的验收结论。

评审时 · 实际观察

verify-3 QA 结果 endedAt=1791158027593、status=completed；repair-3 结果 startedAt=1791158181347，晚于 verify-3，且以 status=failed（Upstream stream ended before terminal chunk）结束。快照已含 repair-3 写入的 client/pages/materials 与路由，但无 verify-4，故 QA 全失败对应缺失 materials UI 的旧状态。

实际影响
把 verify-3 全失败当作框架或最终应用结论会误判；B01–B06 不应据此扣框架分。
建议改法
修复轮完成后重跑 QA（如 verify-4）并以该结果为准；若修复流中途失败，应明确报告“最终未验收”而非复用旧 QA。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
repair-3 流中断后复用 verify-3 QA 作为最终验收
应有能力 / 行为
修复轮完成后重新执行 QA，或明确标注最终状态未验证
实际观察
verify-3 早于 repair-3，repair-3 以流中断 failed 结束且无后续验收记录
应用绕行与代价
评审只能把 QA 结果标注为修复前快照，不用作框架结论
改进后的验收标准
存在晚于 repair-3 的 QA 记录，或工厂报告明确标注最终未验收。

Project materials frontend and provider registration are missing

未解决

server/providers/materials.ts, the migration and the seed exist, but server/providers/index.ts does not register MaterialServiceProvider, server/routes/index.ts is still empty, client/routes.ts has no materials route, and client/pages/materials/ does not exist.

原因（实现者自述）
The implementation invocation ended (completion=event) after writing only the migration, seed and server provider; its transcript repeatedly planned client/pages/materials/{index,new,detail/index}.tsx but never wrote them.
处理方式
Not addressed in this repair round; it is outside the observed typecheck failure. Needs the next verification stage to surface concrete failures.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

评审源产物：Run 37238852759 / attempt 1。发布重试不改变评审源产物编号。

本次需求中，NocoBase3 在文件插件/Drive、db Repository/Policy、认证授权与 app-server 路由及对应 Skill 上提供了可用的公开能力：上传-私有存储-字节路由、按 owner 的 Policy 作用域与迁移式建表都能按指引组合实现，未发现框架在本次职责内的核心能力缺口。主要问题有两条非核心缺陷线索：@nocobase/db 的 DatabaseManager 与 DatabaseConnection 的 repository 签名不一致，按直觉装配会触发 TS2345；Registry 图片预览基线不反馈解码失败（B06），应用副本自行补了 onError。更关键的是过程证据：最终 QA（verify-3）在 repair-3 之前完成，而 repair-3 以 “Upstream stream ended before terminal chunk” 失败，快照中的 materials 页面从未被验收，因此 B01–B06 全失败不能作为框架结论，本报告只据冻结源码与指引评分。

轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 3全量浏览器 QA报告未通过B01 甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

B05 乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。
B06 使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md

⌄

routes.js 注释与 unwritablePolicy 说明公共字节路由按设计不加认证；SKILL 第 122–126 行给出逐路径注册应用认证、禁止 router.use("*") 的做法，app 据此用 securedFileRoutes 包装。

E1 E23 E28
做得好的地方
F5 · 上传 Policy 继承 scope/defaults，读取 filter 与 Policy scope 求交

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · @nocobase/db

⌄

uploadPolicy 在 create:false 时保持 false，否则继承 create.scope/defaults 并把字段替换为 FILE_COLUMNS；combinePolicyFilter 以 and 组合调用方 filter 与 Policy scope，使 owner 过滤既不能越权也不能被覆盖。

应保留的能力

证据与原文

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:11–27

unwritablePolicy 把 read/create/update/delete 全部置 false，注释说明这是公共字节路由与内容 URL 装饰的基 Policy；紧接着是 defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes，并标注 “Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned.”

 * The base Policy for the paths that neither write nor scope what they read:
 * the public byte route, and the middleware that decorates a CRUD response
 * with a content URL.
 *
 * `uploadPolicy` reads every file column whatever base it is handed, and none
 * of these paths writes, so granting nothing is the honest base. It also keeps
 * the application's principal resolver from running a second time on a request
 * the CRUD routes have already resolved it for.
 */
const unwritablePolicy = {
    read: false,
    create: false,
    update: false,
    delete: false,
};
/** Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned. */
export function defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes(options) {

原文件 SHA-256：6982c2d56153fe60426c48783c0653f4224691075e1c95a3d6e39f7405a46025

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:192–231

内容路由 GET <accessPath>/:file：UUID+可选扩展名严格正则、Cache-Control private,no-store、redirect 模式的最终目标同源校验、Content-Type/Content-Length/nosniff/CSP/Content-Disposition 头，随后以 Readable.toWeb 流式返回字节。

                router.get(`${entry.accessPath}/:file`, async (c) => {
                    const match = /^([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})(?:\.([a-z0-9]{1,32}))?$/.exec(c.req.param('file'));
                    if (!match)
                        return c.notFound();
                    const record = await files.findOne({ filter: { id: match[1] } });
                    if (!record || record.ext !== (match[2] ?? ''))
                        return c.notFound();
                    const disk = app.container
                        .resolve(driveManagerToken)
                        .use(record.disk);
                    if (!(await disk.exists(record.key)))
                        return c.notFound();
                    c.header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store');
                    if (entry.accessMode === 'redirect') {
                        const url = await files.getStorageUrl(record);
                        const publicBasePath = app.publicBasePath ?? '';
                        // Check the final target after the host rewrites root-relative redirects.
                        const location = new URL(addBasePathToLocation(url, publicBasePath), c.req.url);
                        if (location.origin === new URL(c.req.url).origin &&
                            entries.some((item) => location.pathname.startsWith(`${joinBasePath(publicBasePath, item.accessPath)}/`)))
                            throw new FileRepositoryError('STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE', 'Storage URL points back to a file access route.');
                        return c.redirect(url, 302);
                    }
                    c.header('Content-Type', validMime(record.mimeType));
                    c.header('Content-Length', String(record.size));
                    c.header('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
                    c.header('Content-Security-Policy', "sandbox; default-src 'none'");
                    c.header('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent(Buffer.from(record.filename).toString('utf8')).replace(/['()*]/g, (char) => `%${char.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()}`)}`);
                    return c.body(Readable.toWeb(await disk.getStream(record.key)));
                });
            }
            return router;
        }),
    ];
}
function fileRouter() {
    const router = new Hono();
    router.onError((error, c) => {
        if (error instanceof HTTPException)
            return error.getResponse();

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/repository.js:25–49

uploadPolicy 派生的 create：base.create 为 true/false 时保持；否则继承 create.scope/defaults 并把字段替换为 FILE_COLUMNS、relations 置空。read 固定 scope:true + 全文件列，update/delete 为 false。

 * input: which rows this principal may produce (`scope`), and what the server
 * stamps onto them (`defaults`). A `create` that is `false` stays `false`, so
 * an exposure that forbids creating rows forbids uploading them too.
 *
 * This is the only place a Policy is widened, and the reason is specific to
 * this path. It licenses nothing elsewhere.
 */
export function uploadPolicy(base) {
    const create = base.create === true || base.create === false
        ? base.create
        : {
            scope: base.create.scope,
            defaults: base.create.defaults,
            fields: FILE_COLUMNS,
            relations: {},
        };
    // `read` serves this path's own returning record and the public byte route,
    // both of which need every column. `update` and `delete` never run here.
    return {
        create,
        read: { scope: true, fields: FILE_COLUMNS, relations: {} },
        update: false,
        delete: false,
    };
}

E5 · packages/@nocobase/drive/dist/prepare.js:1–10

prepareDriveStorage 遍历 disks、筛出 driver=fs 的 location，并 mkdir({recursive:true}) 预建目录；说明私有磁盘只需 location 即可在启动时准备。

import { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
export async function prepareDriveStorage(config) {
    const directories = Array.from(new Set(Object.values(config.disks)
        .filter((disk) => disk.driver === 'fs')
        .map((disk) => disk.location)));
    for (const directory of directories) {
        await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
    }
    return { directories };
}

E7 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/repository-routes.d.ts:27–59

RepositoryApiExposure 要求每个 exposure 声明 policy（RepositoryPolicy 或 principal 函数）；注释说明缺失声明等于接受一切，principal(context) 缺失时返回 undefined 即 403，且认证归应用所有。

export interface RepositoryApiExposure<P = unknown> {
    /** The name passed to api.repository(name). */
    readonly name: string;
    /** Logical Collection name; defaults to name. */
    readonly collection?: string;
    /** Database connection name; defaults to the application's default connection. */
    readonly connection?: string;
    /**
     * The Policy every action of this exposure runs under. Required: an exposure
     * without one would accept anything, and a missing declaration is the one
     * mistake that reads exactly like a deliberate one.
     *
     * A function receives the principal resolved for the request, which is what
     * lets a scope name the caller. It is server-owned either way — a request
     * body naming `policy` or `scope` is refused, because the per-action
     * allowlist below enumerates what an action reads from the body and neither
     * is on it.
     */
    readonly policy: RepositoryPolicy | ((principal: P) => RepositoryPolicy);
    readonly actions: RepositoryApiActions;
}
export interface DefineRepositoryApiRoutesOptions<P = unknown> {
    readonly repositories: readonly RepositoryApiExposure<P>[];
    /**
     * Resolve the principal a Policy function receives. Required as soon as one
     * exposure declares a Policy function, and never called otherwise.
     *
     * The application owns this: `app-server` does not know how a request is
     * authenticated. Returning `undefined` or `null` refuses the request with
     * 403 rather than binding a Policy built from a principal that is not there,
     * which is why the return type admits both.
     */
    readonly principal?: (context: Context) => P | undefined | null | Promise<P | undefined | null>;

    withPolicy(policy, principal) {
        // A Repository handed out by a policy-bound Connection is still a
        // DefaultRepository, and the Connection types it as `Repository`, so this
        // method stays reachable. Replacing the binding there would hand back an
        // unrestricted Repository and undo the Connection's authorization, so
        // rebinding is refused rather than allowed to widen. Tightening further
        // is what `narrow` is for.
        if (this.options.policy) {
            invalid('INVALID_POLICY', 'This Repository already has a bound Policy. Use narrow() to tighten it; a Policy cannot be replaced.', { collection: this.options.collection });
        }
        const input = typeof policy === 'function' ? policy(principal) : policy;
        return new DefaultRepository({
            ...this.options,
            policy: normalizeRepositoryPolicy(input),
        });
    }

E18 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/policy/enforce.js:66–81

combinePolicyFilter 在调用方 filter 与 Policy scope 都存在时返回 logic:and 的分组根，把 caller.root 与 policy.root 并列，不扁平化也不覆盖任一侧。

/** Intersect the caller filter with a Policy scope without flattening either side. */
export function combinePolicyFilter(caller, policy, collection) {
    if (!caller)
        return policy;
    if (!policy)
        return caller;
    return {
        kind: 'filter',
        version: 1,
        collection,
        root: {
            kind: 'group',
            logic: 'and',
            items: [caller.root, policy.root],
        },
    };

async function fetchOfficeOpenXml(
  url: string,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  t: ReturnType<typeof useTranslation>['t'],
): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    credentials: fileUrlCredentials(url),
    signal,
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new OfficeOpenXmlRequestError(
      t('files.previewRequestFailed', {
        defaultValue: `Preview request failed (${response.status}).`,
        status: response.status,
      }),
    );
  }
  return await response.arrayBuffer();
}

E21 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/lib/file-preview.ts:111–138

resolveFilePreviewKind 按 MIME/扩展名分类：活动内容(MIME+XML/ACTIVE_*)判为 unsupported，再依次 markdown/ooxml/office/image/pdf/audio/video/text，未识别返回 unsupported；不做内容嗅探。

export function resolveFilePreviewKind(file: FileRecord): FilePreviewKind {
  const mimeType = file.mimeType.split(';', 1)[0]?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '';
  const extension = fileExtension(file.filename);
  if (
    ACTIVE_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType) ||
    mimeType.endsWith('+xml') ||
    ACTIVE_EXTENSIONS.has(extension)
  ) {
    return 'unsupported';
  }
  if (mimeType === 'text/markdown' || extension === '.md') return 'markdown';
  if (resolveOfficeOpenXmlFormat(file)) return 'ooxml';
  if (OFFICE_MIME_TYPES.has(mimeType) || OFFICE_EXTENSIONS.has(extension)) {
    return 'office';
  }
  if (isSafeImagePreview(file)) return 'image';
  if (mimeType === 'application/pdf' || extension === '.pdf') return 'pdf';
  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/')) return 'audio';
  if (mimeType.startsWith('video/')) return 'video';
  if (
    mimeType.startsWith('text/') ||
    mimeType === 'application/json' ||
    extension === '.json'
  ) {
    return 'text';
  }
  return 'unsupported';
}

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:122–126

SKILL 说明 upload 继承 create.scope/defaults、create:false 亦禁止上传；内容路由是 public 且 Policy 不覆盖；明确要求应用对每个自有路径（/<name>:<action>、<accessPath>/*）注册 App 拥有的认证/授权，禁止 router.use("*")。

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata, and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>:<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

原文件 SHA-256：06b464d71554a3ae12819918f98a84f44c65c2b70e7fbfcffd17e992e57aed22

E24 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:248–253

SKILL 列出错误码恢复（BODY_TOO_LARGE/INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION/STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE 等），并声明没有内建路由认证、行级 ACL、Range/206、ETag、条件下载、断点续传、物理清理、内容嗅探或病毒扫描，需业务自行实现。

- BODY_TOO_LARGE (413): reduce request size or adjust route limits. INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILES (400): send native File values and a nonempty batch.
- INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION: first compare the migration name, route collection and Server repository argument; then await collections.get(logicalName) and check the active connection and generated metadata. SQLite physical text does not imply a broken datetime definition. Fix the specific lookup, metadata or schema problem and retry the same upload; do not bypass File Repository. Use a new migration for corrections to already-merged history.
- STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE: configure a capable disk or stream mode.
- FILE_COMMIT_UNCERTAIN / FILE_CLEANUP_FAILED: reconcile database records and stored objects before retrying; uploads have no idempotency key.

There is no built-in route authentication, row ACL, Range/206, ETag, conditional download, resumable upload, physical cleanup, content sniffing or malware scan. Metadata deletion retains objects; cancelled forms may leave unlinked files. Implement the policies required by the business, including referenced-file deletion and orphan cleanup.

E25 · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:166–183

SKILL 规定 builder 参数为逻辑名、物理名由 underscored/tablePrefix 生成，提醒 db.builder() 是方法而 connection.builder 是属性；并给出 Migration(builder/query) 与 Seed(repository/query) 的工具与默认值表。

Change an existing table with explicit alter, rename and drop operations in a safe dependency order. Do not drop and recreate — that destroys data and is not what `down()` should undo.

Builder arguments are **logical names**. Physical table and column names are generated deterministically from `underscored` and `tablePrefix`; never store or concatenate `tableName` and `columnName` yourself. The exception is a raw index or constraint name such as `fk_order_items_order`, which is passed through unconverted and must be spelled the same way when dropped.

`db.builder()` is a method on the manager; `connection.builder` is a property. Getting that backwards is the most common typo in this area.

## 4. Repository and Query

Two layers over the same tables. The choice is not stylistic, and two questions settle almost every case.

**Where does the code run?** Runtime code under `server/` reaches both through the manager. A migration and a seed reach both through their own context — as `context.repository(name)`, a method on the context rather than on the minimal task `connection` — but with opposite defaults, and those defaults are the rule rather than a hint:

| Context   | Tools                            | Default for data |
| --------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------- |
| Migration | `builder`, `query`, `repository` | `query`          |
| Seed      | `repository`, `query`            | `repository`     |

A migration changes structure, so `builder` and `query` are its tools and `repository` is reserved for the writes `query` would get wrong: cross-dialect field encoding and decoding, Collection-level naming overrides, and relation writes including the junction rows behind a `belongsToMany`. **Anything `query` expresses correctly stays on `query`.** A seed changes no structure and writes installation data in Collection terms, so `repository` is its normal tool and `query` covers what that cannot express, such as reading a physical table backing no Collection.

原文件 SHA-256：f97c07a45a9d49c1d933ddbceeabcc9afe267b1356a2ded1a3da0dd56b54011c

E26 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/SKILL.md:160–173

Non-negotiables：每条服务端路由自带认证/授权、迁移是不可变且自包含的历史、可见字符串走翻译键、页面容器归属、视觉一致性、路由不含 base path、导航需在 routes.ts 声明、优先内建机制、测试置于 tests/e2e。

## Non-negotiables

These cause real damage and appear in every reference:

- **Every server route owns its own authentication and authorization.** Mounting under `/api` authenticates nothing.
- **A migration is immutable history and self-contained.** Never import an evolving definition into one. Never edit one whose branch is merged.
- **Every user-visible string goes through a translation key.**
- **Let the owning page supply `PageContainer`.** Use `PageContainer` from `@/components/page-container` for shared page padding and spacing. Inline child pages, including Tab content, render inside the parent page's container and must not add another. A covering child page uses its own `PageContainer` inside `RouteChildPage`; dialog and drawer content uses the corresponding overlay container. See [styling](references/frontend/references/styling.md).
- **Visual consistency is application-wide.** Restyling only your part is a defect. Change the design tokens if a change is needed.
- **Route paths never include the deployment base path.** The runtime restores it.
- **Route navigation creates sidebar entries.** Declare `navigation` in `client/routes.ts`; Refine resources are for CRUD, not menus.
- **Reach for the built-in mechanism first.** Changing framework structure is allowed when nothing else fits — comment it and update the docs.
- **Tests live in `tests/` or `e2e/`,** never beside the source.
- **Remove direct NocoBase packages with `package remove` after reviewing their usage.** Do not hand-delete only the manifest entry or leave synchronized Skills and plugin registrations behind.

E28 · app/server/routes/materials.ts:189–226

应用 securedFileRoutes 把 File Repository 贡献包进带 AuthEnv 的 Hono：api 作用域对 :uploadOne 加 auth.required() 与 acceptSupportedFiles；root 作用域对 <accessPath>/:file 加 auth.required() 与 ownFileOnly(service)，注释指出插件路由按契约公开、认证由应用补。

/**
 * Wraps the File Repository contributions so the application's authentication
 * covers them. The plugin's own routes are public by contract; nothing outside
 * this wrapper reaches them.
 */
function securedFileRoutes(
  contributions: FileRoutes,
): AppRouteContribution<Application>[] {
  return contributions.map(
    (contribution): AppRouteContribution<Application> => {
      const createRouter = async (app: Application): Promise<Hono> => {
        const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
        const service = app.container.resolve(materialServiceToken);
        const secured = new Hono<AuthEnv>();
        if (contribution.scope === 'api') {
          secured.use(`/${MATERIAL_FILES_RESOURCE}:uploadOne`, auth.required());
          secured.use(
            `/${MATERIAL_FILES_RESOURCE}:uploadOne`,
            acceptSupportedFiles,
          );
        } else {
          secured.use(`${MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/:file`, auth.required());
          secured.use(
            `${MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/:file`,
            ownFileOnly(service),
          );
        }
        secured.route('/', await contribution.createRouter(app));
        // Hono's router is invariant in its Env, while a contribution's factory
        // promises the blank environment. The middleware above is what carries
        // `AuthEnv`, so the assertion is only about that difference.
        return secured as unknown as Hono;
      };
      return contribution.scope === 'api'
        ? { scope: 'api', createRouter }
        : { scope: 'root', createRouter };
    },
  );

E29 · app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:96–131

应用扩展副本新增 ImagePreview：useState(failed)+<img onError={()=>setFailed(true)}>，失败时渲染 DownloadFallback 并给出 previewFailed 文案，弥补注册表基线图片分支缺少解码失败反馈。

/**
 * An image preview that reports a decode failure instead of leaving a broken
 * image behind. A file can be stored with an image MIME type and valid PNG
 * header while its bytes are corrupt, so `onError` is the only signal that the
 * content cannot be shown; it turns that into the same explicit message and
 * download fallback the other unrenderable kinds already use.
 */
function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
  readonly onDownload?: () => void;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url, onDownload } = inputProps;
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  if (failed || !url) {
    return (
      <DownloadFallback
        file={file}
        message={t('previewFailed', {
          defaultValue: 'Unable to load the file preview.',
        })}
        onDownload={onDownload}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}

    disks,
  };
});

E33 · app/database/main/seeds/202610200002_materials_demo_data.ts:139–190

应用 seed 安装 materials.owner 与 materials.viewer 两个账号、两份虚构资料及文件，文件行写入 createdById=ownerId；数据以 owner 归属落库，演示按 owner 隔离。

  name: '202610200002_materials_demo_data',
  async run(context) {
    const ownerId = await ensureAccount(context, OWNER);
    await ensureAccount(context, VIEWER);

    const materials = context.repository<MaterialRow>('project_materials');
    const materialIds: number[] = [];
    for (const material of MATERIALS) {
      materialIds.push(await ensureMaterial(materials, material, ownerId));
    }

    const location = readLocalDiskLocation(context);
    if (!location) {
      console.warn(
        '[materials] No local filesystem disk is configured for "local"; ' +
          'the demonstration attachments were not installed.',
      );
      return;
    }

    const files = context.repository<FileRow>('project_material_files');
    await mkdir(path.join(location, 'objects'), { recursive: true });
    for (const attachment of ATTACHMENTS) {
      const materialId = materialIds[attachment.materialIndex];
      const existing = await files.findOne({
        filter: { materialId, filename: attachment.filename },
      });
      if (existing) continue;

      const buffer = Buffer.from(attachment.base64, 'base64');
      await writeFile(
        path.join(location, 'objects', `${attachment.id}.${attachment.ext}`),
        buffer,
      );

      const now = new Date();
      await files.createOne({
        values: {
          id: attachment.id,
          disk: 'local',
          key: `objects/${attachment.id}.${attachment.ext}`,
          filename: attachment.filename,
          ext: attachment.ext,
          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
          size: buffer.byteLength,
          createdById: ownerId,
          materialId,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        },
      });
    }

E34 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/README.md:1–13

Registry README 说明安装到 client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui、使用 ClientFileRepository 与 FileRecord/contentUrl；预览 DOCX/XLSX/PPTX 本地经 @silurus/ooxml，OOXML 失败显示错误与可选下载，另注明无 public/private 记录标志且私有磁盘不授权内容请求。

# File Component UI

This Registry item installs editable App source for upload, list, thumbnail, and preview controls. The default destination is client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui. Import components and UI types from its index.ts.

Pass the public ClientFileRepository returned by the App's clientFileRepositoryManagerToken to FileUploadField as repository. Read-only components consume FileRecord arrays and their contentUrl directly; they require no repository prop. See the Registry components section in the plugin's SKILL.md for installation, dependencies, and a complete React example.

Upload success is controlled by value/onChange. Temporary progress and error items stay internal. Use onStatusChange to block form submission while uploading or after a failure. Upload cancellation forwards AbortSignal; it does not guarantee rollback after a server commit. removeOnDelete removes metadata only. App-owned business relations and physical cleanup remain the App's responsibility.

Preview handles safe raster images, PDF through a fetched local blob, audio/video and text/Markdown. DOCX/XLSX/PPTX render locally with lazily loaded `@silurus/ooxml`; legacy Office and OpenDocument formats retain Office Online, which requires an internet-accessible absolute URL and cannot use the App session. Active HTML/SVG/XML and unsafe URL schemes are rejected. Markdown does not execute raw HTML.

Follow the plugin Skill's Registry installation and preview sections for the exact viewer dependency, Vite dependency-prebundling exclusion, authentication adapter boundaries and acceptance checks. Same-origin content fetches use session cookies; external fetches omit credentials and require CORS. Bearer-only access requires changes to the App-owned source; setting `contentUrl` to an arbitrary blob URL is not supported. OOXML failures show an error and an optional download action; `download={false}` suppresses that action.

There is no public/private record flag or access-token endpoint. The item contains no extension.ts, route, collection, or Server implementation. A private disk does not authorize a content request. Installed copies belong to the App; review upgrades with a three-way merge and preserve customizations. Supply translated labels and adapt additional copy in installed source.

原始验收要求

甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

实际操作

  1. 退出到匿名后以资料员甲（materials.owner / Materials#2026）登录 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/login，成功进入 /main。
  2. 在甲的工作台查询页面上的文件上传控件数量，并统计正文是否出现“资料/附件/material”等业务字样。
  3. 以资料员甲身份在浏览器内请求 /api/project_materials:list 与 /api/project_material_files:list。

观察结果

  1. 甲登录成功（右上角头像首字“资”），但 /main 顶部导航只有 Home，页面没有任何“项目资料/附件”业务入口，input[type=file] 数量为 0。
  2. /api/project_materials:list 返回 HTTP 404 {"error":"Not found"}，project_material_files:list 同样 404，业务集合接口不存在。
  3. 没有可用的资料新建/上传界面，因此无法上传 PNG、保存资料、刷新后查看真实图片，也无法下载打开。

乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。

实际操作

  1. 以普通同事乙（materials.viewer / Materials#2026）登录，进入 /main 并检查业务导航，再请求 /api/project_materials:list。
  2. 清除 Cookie 成为未登录访问者，打开 /main 观察跳转，并以匿名请求 /api/project_materials:list。

  2. 未登录访问 /main 被重定向到 /main/login，匿名请求 /api/project_materials:list 同样返回 404。
